Pair-based coordinate descent:
- For a given pair (i,j), derive A_δ for all lags: coefficient of t. For each pair of indices p,q (p first, q second) contributing to lag δ, old contributions old_p old? Let's derive coefficient when h_i = t, h_j = m - t.
  For any term h_p(1-h_q), if p=i and q≠i,j: coeff + (1 - h_q). If p=j: coeff (1 - h_q)*(dh_j/dt=-1) -> - (1 - h_q). If p not i,j, q=i: coeff - h_p. If q=j: coeff - h_p * (-1)= + h_p. If p=q=δ=0: terms h_i(1-h_i) and h_j(1-h_j) quadratic; handle separately.
  We can compute Aδ by looping over affected lags (all lags involving i or j) which is O(n) each, total O(n^2) per pair. If we do many pairs maybe expensive. Implement top-level `prepare_pair_data(i,j,h,q,first_idx,second_idx,n,offset)`:
```
L=2*n-1
B = q.copy()
A = np.zeros(L)
oi = h[i]; oj = h[j]; m=oi+oj
q0_base = q[offset] - oi*(1-oi) - oj*(1-oj)
B[offset] = q0_base  # before direct? q0 only self; other groups won't touch; direct no. But if we later set -inf.
# p=i first
qs = np.arange(n)  # global
non_q0 = qs != i
idxs = first_idx[i][non_q0]
vals = oi * (1.0 - h[qs[non_q0]])
B[idxs] -= vals
non_dir = non_q0 & (qs != j)
idxs2 = first_idx[i][non_dir]
A[idxs2] += 1.0 - h[qs[non_dir]]
# p=j first
non_q0j = qs != j
idxsj = first_idx[j][non_q0j]
valsj = oj * (1.0 - h[qs[non_q0j]])
B[idxsj] -= valsj
non_dirj = non_q0j & (qs != i)
idxsj2 = first_idx[j][non_dirj]
oneh = 1.0 - h[qs[non_dirj]]
B[idxsj2] += m * oneh
A[idxsj2] -= oneh
# q=i second p not i,j
p_mask = (qs != i) & (qs != j)
hp = h[qs[p_mask]]
idxsi = second_idx[i][p_mask]
oldi = hp * (1.0 - oi)
B[idxsi] -= oldi
B[idxsi] += hp
A[idxsi] -= hp
# q=j second p not i,j
idxsjd = second_idx[j][p_mask]
oldj = hp * (1.0 - oj)
B[idxsjd] -= oldj
B[idxsjd] += hp * (1.0 - m)
A[idxsjd] += hp
# after all, B[offset] should still q0_base. Ensure no duplicate addition changed offset? Use B[offset] = q0_base
B[offset] = q0_base
B[offset] = -np.inf
return B,A,m,q0_base, lo,hi,d1,d2

  # assume f(lo) >0, f(hi)<0

    mid=(lo+hi)/2
    if eval_f(mid) > 0: lo=mid
    else: hi=mid
  return (lo+hi)/2

Implementation details per run:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = None; best_c5 = inf; best_n=0
    try parent...
    if parent...: setup n=95, h=parent, q=build_q, c=...
    else: start n=96 with random feasible.
    # optimize primary n
    h_opt, q_opt, maxsum_opt = optimize_pair_n(h, n, rng, start, budget, ...)
    update best if maxsum*2/n < best_c5
    # maybe optimize other n?
```

second_idx = [offset + i - np.arange(n, dtype=int) for i in range(n)]
pair_i, pair_j = np.triu_indices(n, k=1)
qs = np.arange(n)
```

`optimize_pairs(...)`:
```
def optimize_pairs(h, q, n, precomp, rng, max_sweeps, start_time, time_limit, tol, verbose=False):
    offset, L, first_idx, second_idx, pair_i, pair_j, qs = precomp
    dx = 2.0/n
    maxsum = float(np.max(q))
    best_maxsum = maxsum
    best_h = h.copy(); best_q = q.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    pair_count = len(pair_i)
    sweeps = 0
    while time remaining:
       sweeps +=1
       improved = False
       # choose order: if sweeps%2 determin? Use random permutation for stochastics; maybe early sorted by m? 
       order = rng.permutation(pair_count)
       for idx in order:
          if time.time() - start_time > time_limit: break
          i = pair_i[idx]; j = pair_j[idx]
          oi=h[i]; oj=h[j]; m=oi+oj
          lo = max(0.0, m-1.0); hi=min(1.0,m)
          if hi-lo < 1e-12: continue
          data = prepare_pair_data(i,j,h,q,first_idx,second_idx,qs,offset)
          t_best, val_best = choose_t(..., lo,hi, oi maybe)
          if val_best < maxsum - 1e-11:
              apply_update...
              maxsum = new_maxsum
              if maxsum < best_maxsum - ...:
                   best_maxsum=...; best_h=h.copy(); best_q=q.copy()
              improved=True
          # else no
       if improved: no_improve=0 else no_improve+=1
       if no_improve >= 3: break? Could continue random restarts outside.
       # periodic fix sum and recompute q if drift
       if sweeps %5==0: fix_sum_project? recompute q? 
    return best_h,best_q,best_maxsum
```
But `prepare_pair_data` allocates B,A each pair; if no improvement, still. Time.
Need choose `time_limit` not full budget; leave time for final evaluation.
For primary n, allow maybe 80% budget. For other starts, remaining.

However returning best_h,q. Need if best_c5 update. After optimize, apply final fix.

`choose_t` top-level:

Projection to sum exactly:
```
def project_sum(h, target):
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # use bisection on theta: h_proj=clip(h - theta,0,1); solve sum=target
    lo=min(h)-1, hi=max(h)
    for _ in range(50): mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0,1)); if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    theta=(lo+hi)/2
    h=np.clip(h-theta,0,1)
    # fix residual by adjusting fractional
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       mask=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
       if mask.sum()>0: h[mask] += diff/mask.sum(); h=np.clip...
    return h
```

Detailed function signatures:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5_numpy(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    if n==0: return 1.0
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr))*dx

def build_q(h, n, offset):
    L=2*n-1
    q=np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    one_minus = 1.0 - h
    # for p 0..n-1 slice
    for p in range(n):
        hp = h[p]
        if hp != 0.0:
            q[offset - p : offset - p + n] += hp * one_minus
    return q
```
Check slice length: offset-p+n. For p=0: offset+n = 2n-1 valid stop exclusive L. For p=n-1: offset-(n-1)+n = 2n-2? Wait offset=n-1, stop = n-1 - (n-1) + n = n, length n from 0 to n; but L=2n-1, okay. For p=n-1, indices 0..n-1? q = p? δ = q-p ranges -p..n-1-p: for p=n-1 => -n+1..0; indices offset+δ =0..n-1. stop exclusive n; length n. Good.
This builds q[δ] where δ=q-p. Yes.

def make_precomp(n):
    offset=n-1; L=2*n-1
    qs=np.arange(n,dtype=int)
    first_idx=[np.arange(offset-i, offset-i+n, dtype=int) for i in range(n)]
    second_idx=[offset+i-np.arange(n, dtype=int) for i in range(n)]
    pair_i, pair_j = np.triu_indices(n, k=1, dtype=int)
    return (offset,L,first_idx,second_idx,pair_i,pair_j,qs)
```

```

    non_j = qs != j
    both = non_i & non_j
    # p=i first
    idx = first_idx[i][non_i]
    vals = oi * (1.0 - h[non_i])
    B[idx] -= vals

    # p=j first

    maskj = non_i & non_j
    idxj2 = first_idx[j][maskj]

    B[idxj2] += m * onh

    # second

    B[idxi] -= oldi
    B[idxi] += hp
    A[idxi] -= hp
    idxj = second_idx[j][both]

`eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset)`:
```
def eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset):
    vals = B + A * t
    # offset is -inf; direct adds finite
    v = t * (1.0 - m + t)
    vals[d1] += v
    v2 = (m - t) * (1.0 - t)
    vals[d2] += v2
    return float(np.max(vals))
```

`eval_R_scalar(t,m,q0_base)`:

     b=d

     a=c

def bisection_f_left(lo, hi, ...): # f(lo)>0, f(hi)<=0

```
def fix_sum_exact(h, target):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n=len(h)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: return h
    # Try to adjust one fractional coordinate
    frac = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1-1e-12)
    idx = np.where(frac)[0]
    if len(idx) > 0:
       # adjust the one with enough room? For small diff, choose idx with max slack in required direction
       if diff > 0:
         # need increase; choose index with largest 1-h
         order = idx[np.argsort(-(1-h[idx]))]
       else:
         order = idx[np.argsort(h[idx])]
       amount = diff
       for k in order:
          if amount == 0: break
          room = 1-h[k] if diff>0 else h[k]
          step = amount if amount>0 else amount
          # clip step to room with sign
          max_step = room if amount>0 else -room
          actual = max(min(amount, max_step), min(amount, -max_step)?)
```
Simpler vector projected sum: Use threshold projection which gives exact within numerical. But final may not be exact enough. Since pair sum nearly exact, diff tiny, one fractional adjust enough.
Implement robust:
```
    diff = target - sum
    if abs(diff) > 0:
        # if diff positive, add to coordinates not at 1; if many, distribute to largest slack first
        if diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]
            for k in order:
                if diff <= 1e-14: break
                room = 1-h[k]
                if room <= 0: continue
                add = min(diff, room)
                h[k] += add; diff -= add
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            for k in order:
                if -diff <= 1e-14: break
                room = h[k]
                if room <=0: continue
                sub = min(-diff, room)
                h[k] -= sub; diff += sub
    # Round tiny to 0/1
    h[np.abs(h)<1e-14]=0; h[np.abs(h-1)<1e-14]=1
    # final if not close due to float, use projection threshold and re-fix
    if abs(target - sum(h)) > 1e-10:
        h = project_sum(h,target)
        h = adjust exact...
    return h
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + float(budget_s)
    safety = deadline - 5
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = None; best_c5 = inf; best_n=0; best_q=None
    # global best update function? No closures; write inline or helper `update_best`? It would need nonlocal not allowed? We can just variables.
    # obtain parent
    parent_h = None
    try:
        parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None
    if parent_h is not None and len(parent_h)>0:
        n = len(parent_h)
        target = n/2.0
        h = fix_sum_exact(parent_h, target)
        pre = make_precomp(n)
        q = build_q(h,n,pre[0])
        c = float(np.max(q))*2.0/n
        best_h=h; best_c5=c; best_n=n; best_q=q
        # primary optimize
        h_opt, q_opt, maxsum_opt = optimize_pairs(h,q,n,pre,rng,start, min(safety, start + max(120, budget_s*0.55)), ...)
        c_opt = maxsum_opt*2.0/n
        if c_opt < best_c5: update
        # if time, perturb/optimize from best
        # if time, resample n2
    else:
        # create n=96 candidates
        for seed/cand in ... optimize short maybe
```

`project_sum(h, target)`:
```
def project_sum(h, target):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return h
    x=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    target=float(target)
    # if sum close return
    s=x.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<=1e-12: return x
    lo=float(x.min()); hi=float(x.max())
    # find theta such that sum clip(x-theta)=target
    a=lo-1.0; b=hi
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=0.5*(a+b)
        val=np.sum(np.clip(x-mid,0,1))
        if val > target:
            a=mid
        else:
            b=mid
    theta=0.5*(a+b)
    x=np.clip(x-theta,0,1)
    # fix residual
    diff=target - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            order=np.argsort(1.0-x)[::-1]
        else:
            order=np.argsort(x)
        for k in order:
            if diff > 0:
                room=1.0-x[k]
                if room <=1e-15: continue
                add=min(diff, room)
                x[k]+=add; diff-=add
            else:
                room=x[k]
                if room <=1e-15: continue
                sub=min(-diff, room)
                x[k]-=sub; diff+=sub
            if abs(diff)<=1e-14: break
    x[x<0 & x<1e-12? syntax] =0.0
    x[(x>1-1e-12) & (x<1)] =1.0? Actually `x[np.abs(x) < 1e-13] = 0`; `x[np.abs(x-1)<1e-13]=1`.
    return x
```
But `x[x<0 & x<1e-12]` precedence wrong. Use `x[np.abs(x) < 1e-13] = 0.0; x[np.abs(x - 1.0) < 1e-13] = 1.0`.
`fix_sum_exact` can just call project_sum then adjust one. Maybe one function `make_feasible(h,target)` does project and exact adjust. Use for all.

`add_perturb(h, rng, amp)`:
```
def add_perturb(h, rng, amp):
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0
    h2=h + amp*rng.normal(size=n)
    return project_sum(h2,target)
```
`random_binary_h(n,rng)`:
```
h=np.zeros(n); idx=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(idx); k=n//2; h[idx[:k]]=1; if n%2==1: h[idx[k]]=0.5; return h
```
`smooth_initial_h(n,rng)`:
```
x=np.arange(n); h=0.5+0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*x/n*(1+rng.rand())+rng.rand()*2*np.pi); return project_sum(np.clip(h,0,1), n/2)
```

def choose_pair_t(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset, t_old):
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return lo, eval_full_scalar(lo, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
    tP, Pv = ternary_P_min(lo, hi, B,A,m,d1,d2,offset)
    Rmax = q0_base + m - 0.5*m*m
    cands = [lo, hi, tP]
    if Rmax < Pv - 1e-10:
        t = tP
    else:
        tf, fmin = ternary_f_min(lo, hi, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
        cands.append(tf)
        if fmin < -1e-9:
            f_lo = eval_f_scalar(lo, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
            if f_lo > 0:
                left = bisection_f_left(lo, tf, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
            else:
                left = lo
            f_hi = eval_f_scalar(hi, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
            if f_hi > 0:
                right = bisection_f_right(tf, hi, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
            else:
                right = hi
            cands.extend([left, right])
        t = None
    best_t = tP
    best_val = eval_full_scalar(tP,...)
    for t in cands: ...
    # t_old candidate
    if lo <= t_old <= hi: evaluate
    # local grid around best_t:
    span = hi - lo
    if span > 1e-12:
       for frac in (-0.25,-0.1,0.1,0.25, -0.5,0.5):
          t2=best_t + frac*span
          if lo<=t2<=hi: evaluate
    return best_t,best_val
```
If Rmax<Pv, cands [lo,hi,tP]; tP best. Adding grid. Good.

```
f_tf = fmin
if f_lo > 0 and f_tf <= 0: bisection else left = lo if f_lo<=0 else tf
```

def try_candidate(h_in, n, rng, time_limit):
    h_in = np.array(h_in, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n = int(len(h_in))
    if n <= 1:
       c=compute_c5_numpy(h_in); return h_in,c,n
    target=0.5*n
    h_feas=make_feasible(h_in,target)
    pre=make_precomp(n)
    q=build_q(h_feas,n,pre[0])
    h_opt,q_opt,maxsum=optimize_pairs(h_feas,q,n,pre,rng,float(time_limit))
    h_opt=make_feasible(h_opt, target)
    c=compute_c5_numpy(h_opt)
    return h_opt,c,n
```

Now, `make_feasible` uses `project_sum`. Let's implement robustly.
```
def make_feasible(h, target):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return h
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)
    # first simple projection
    h = project_sum(h, target)
    # one or two exact corrections
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    # if diff small, adjust single values making dyadic? 
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       if diff > 0:
          order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
          for k in order:
             room = 1.0 - h[k]
             if room <= 1e-15: continue
             add = min(diff, room)
             h[k] += add
             diff -= add
             if abs(diff) <= 1e-15: break
       else:
          order = np.argsort(h)
          for k in order:
             room = h[k]
             if room <= 1e-15: continue
             sub = min(-diff, room)
             h[k] -= sub
             diff += sub
             if abs(diff) <= 1e-15: break
    h[np.abs(h) < 1e-14] = 0.0
    h[np.abs(h - 1.0) < 1e-14] = 1.0
    return h
```
This may overshoot due to floating after adding. If diff after loop not zero, project again? Could loop twice. Add:
```
for _ in range(2):
  diff = target - sum; if abs<1e-12 break; adjust
```
Good.

`project_sum` threshold: If target 0 or n? Not but handle. lo=min(h)-1, hi=max(h). If all h 0 and target>0? a=-1, b=0; clip. Works. But bisection for threshold when target > sum possible? The projection sum monotonic decreasing theta. At theta=lo-1, all h? if lo is min h, x - (lo-1) >=1 -> sum n, ≥target. At theta=hi, max clipped maybe <target? if target ≤n. Good. Use a=lo-1, b=hi. If target close, fine.
Could simply use greedy because target near after h initial. But threshold robust.
After threshold, residual small; greedy fix.

Now, `add_perturb` uses project_sum. If h length n, target n/2. Good.

`random_binary_h(n,rng)`:
```
h = np.zeros(n)

rng.shuffle(idx)
k = n//2
h[idx[:k]] = 1.0
if n % 2 == 1:
    h[idx[k]] = 0.5
return h

Need `np` and `time` imported.

Let's draft each.

`compute_c5_numpy`:
```
def compute_c5_numpy(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 1:
        if n == 0:
            return float('inf')
        dx = 2.0
        return float(max(0.0, float(h[0])*(1.0-h[0]))*dx)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)) * (2.0 / n)
```
For n=1, dx=2; C=2h(1-h). But not used. Good.

`build_q`: if n<=1? For n=1, offset=0,L=1; loop p=0 slice [0:1] += hp*(1-h). Works. Good.

`make_precomp` for n=1: `np.triu_indices(1,k=1)` empty. first_idx [np.arange(0,1)], second_idx [0 - np.arange(1) =0]. Good.

`project_sum`:
```
def project_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return h
    target = float(target)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s - target) <= 1e-12:
        return h
    a = float(h.min()) - 1.0
    b = float(h.max())
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(a+b)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if val > target:
            a = mid
        else:
            b = mid
    theta = 0.5*(a+b)
    h = np.clip(h - theta, 0.0, 1.0)
    # residual adjust
    for _ in range(2):
       diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: break
       if diff > 0:
          order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
          for k in order:
             room = 1.0 - h[k]
             if room <= 1e-15: continue
             add = min(diff, room)
             h[k] += add
             diff -= add
             if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: break
       else:
          order = np.argsort(h)
          for k in order:
             room = h[k]
             if room <= 1e-15: continue
             sub = min(-diff, room)
             h[k] -= sub
             diff += sub
             if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: break
    h[np.abs(h) < 1e-13] = 0.0
    h[np.abs(h - 1.0) < 1e-13] = 1.0
    return h
```
If target not reachable due to bounds? n/2 reachable. If projection b/h min issues? okay.

```
def make_feasible(h,target):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if len(h)==0: return h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    h=project_sum(h,target)
    for _ in range(2):
       diff=target-float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff)<=1e-12: break
       # same adjust
    h[np.abs(h)<1e-13]=0; h[np.abs(h-1)<1e-13]=1
    return h
```
Could just call project_sum and return; it already adjusts. `make_feasible` can just `return project_sum(np.asarray... , target)` because project does. But project uses own residual. Let's define make_feasible simply to avoid duplicate:

```

Now, eval functions need use `float` for t? Input float.
```
def eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset):
    vals = B + A * float(t)
    vals[d1] += float(t) * (1.0 - m + float(t))
    vals[d2] += (m - float(t)) * (1.0 - float(t))
    return float(np.max(vals))
```

def eval_P_scalar(...):
    tt = float(t)
    vals = B + A * tt
    vals[d1] += tt * (1.0 - m + tt)
    vals[d2] += (m - tt) * (1.0 - tt)
    return float(np.max(vals))
```

`ternary_P_min`:
```
def ternary_P_min(lo, hi, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset):
    a=float(lo); b=float(hi)
    if b-a < 1e-14:
       t=0.5*(a+b); return t, eval_P_scalar(t,...)
    for _ in range(_P_ITERS):
       c = a + (b-a)/3.0
       d = b - (b-a)/3.0
       if eval_P_scalar(c,...) <= eval_P_scalar(d,...):
          b=d
       else:
          a=c
    t=0.5*(a+b)
    return t, eval_P_scalar(t,...)
```
For convex minimization, if f(c)<f(d), min in [a,d], so b=d. If f(c)>f(d), min in [c,b], a=c. Good.
`ternary_f_min` similar with `eval_f_scalar`.

Bisection:
```
def bisection_f_left(lo, hi, B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset):
    a=float(lo); b=float(hi)
    if eval_f_scalar(a,...) <= 0.0: return a
    if eval_f_scalar(b,...) >= 0.0: return b
    for _ in range(_B_ITERS):
       mid=0.5*(a+b)
       if eval_f_scalar(mid,...) > 0.0:
          a=mid
       else:
          b=mid
    return 0.5*(a+b)
```

```
def bisection_f_right(lo, hi,...): # f(lo)<=0, f(hi)>=0
    a=lo;b=hi
    if eval_f_scalar(a) >=0: return a
    if eval_f_scalar(b) <=0: return b
    for:
       mid
       if eval_f(mid) <=0: a=mid
       else: b=mid

`choose_pair_t` as above. Need use `_EPS` maybe 1e-12.
```
def choose_pair_t(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset, t_old):
    lo=float(lo); hi=float(hi)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
       t=lo; return t, eval_full_scalar(t,...)
    # if interval tiny evaluate few
    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
       best ... among lo,hi,clamped t_old
    tP,Pv=ternary_P_min(...)
    Rmax = q0_base + m - 0.5*m*m
    cands=[lo,hi,tP]
    if Rmax < Pv - 1e-10:
       # no roots; but still include t_old maybe
       cands.append(t_old)
    else:
       tf,fmin=ternary_f_min(...)
       cands.append(tf)
       if fmin < -1e-9:
          f_lo=eval_f_scalar(lo,...)
          if f_lo > 0:
             left = bisection_f_left(lo, tf,...) if fmin <=0 else tf
          else: left=lo
          f_hi=eval_f_scalar(hi,...)
          if f_hi > 0:
             right = bisection_f_right(tf, hi,...) if fmin <=0 else tf
          else: right=hi
          cands.extend([left,right])
       cands.append(t_old)
    # optional midpoint
    cands.append(0.5*(lo+hi))
    best_t=tP; best_val=eval_full_scalar(tP,...)
    for t in cands:
       tt=float(t)
       if tt < lo: tt=lo
       elif tt > hi: tt=hi
       val=eval_full_scalar(tt,...)
       if val < best_val:
          best_val=val; best_t=tt
    # local grid
    span=hi-lo
    if span > 1e-14:
       for frac in (-0.5, -0.25, -0.1, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
          tt=best_t + frac*span
          if tt >= lo and tt <= hi:
             val=eval_full_scalar(tt,...)
             if val < best_val: best...
    return best_t,best_val
```
This includes local grid 6 evals. Good. If Rmax<Pv, no tf; cands includes lo,hi,tP,t_old,mid. good.
If fmin < -1e-9 but f_lo>0 and fmin<=0, bisection. If fmin positive due to number but fmin< -? no.

Could `eval_full_scalar(lo)` with direct values produce NaN if B -inf? P eval for lo excludes offset, direct finite. finite.

`prepare_pair_data` with precomp tuple:
```
def prepare_pair_data(i,j,h,q,precomp):
   offset,L,first_idx,second_idx,pair_i,pair_j,qs,not_masks = precomp
   B=q.copy(); A=np.zeros(L,float)
   oi=float(h[i]); oj=float(h[j]); m=oi+oj
   m = min(2.0, max(0.0, m))
   non_i=not_masks[i]; non_j=not_masks[j]; both = non_i & non_j
   # p=i first
   idx = first_idx[i][non_i]
   vals = oi * (1.0 - h[non_i])
   B[idx] -= vals
   mask = both  # q not i,j
   idx2 = first_idx[i][mask]
   A[idx2] += 1.0 - h[mask]
   # p=j first
   idxj=first_idx[j][non_j]
   valsj=oj*(1.0 - h[non_j])
   B[idxj] -= valsj
   idxj2=first_idx[j][both]
   onh = 1.0 - h[both]
   B[idxj2] += m * onh
   A[idxj2] -= onh
   # second
   hp = h[both]
   idxi = second_idx[i][both]
   oldi = hp * (1.0 - oi)
   B[idxi] -= oldi
   B[idxi] += hp
   A[idxi] -= hp
   idxj2 = second_idx[j][both]
   oldj = hp * (1.0 - oj)
   B[idxj2] -= oldj
   B[idxj2] += hp * (1.0 - m)
   A[idxj2] += hp
   q0_base = float(q[offset]) - oi*(1.0-oi) - oj*(1.0-oj)
   if q0_base < 0 and q0_base > -1e-12: q0_base=0.0? maybe not; keep.
   B[offset] = -np.inf
   d1 = j - i + offset
   d2 = i - j + offset
   lo = m - 1.0
   if lo < 0.0: lo=0.0
   hi = m
   if hi > 1.0: hi=1.0
   if hi < lo: hi=lo
   return B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,lo,hi
```

For p=j first, `onh = 1.0 - h[both]`, where both excludes q=j? Wait for p=j first non-direct q not i,j. `both` is p mask for second? It excludes i and j. For p=j first, q should not be j (q0) and q not i (direct), so q ∈ both (not i,j). Yes `both` identifies q indices that are neither i nor j. But `both` originally from not_i & not_j over qs. Good. Now, `apply_pair_update`:
```
def apply_pair_update(h,q,i,j,t,B,A,m,q0_base,d1,d2,offset):
    tt=float(t)
    hy = m - tt
    # clamp
    if tt < 0.0: tt=0.0; hy=m
    elif tt > 1.0: tt=1.0; hy=m-tt
    if hy < 0.0: hy=0.0; tt=m
    elif hy > 1.0: hy=1.0; tt=m-hy
    h[i]=tt; h[j]=hy
    qt = B + A * tt
    qt[offset] = q0_base + tt*(1.0-tt) + (m - tt)*(1.0 - (m - tt))
    qt[d1] += tt * (1.0 - m + tt)
    qt[d2] += (m - tt) * (1.0 - tt)
    q[:] = qt
    # optional clean tiny negatives
    # maxq = np.max(q)
    return float(np.max(q))
```
If clamping changes tt, should hy=m-clamped; if m was clamped to [0,2], okay. If m was outside and clamped, pair sum changed by tiny; okay. But if due to clamping both h values sum m not old; fine. Need if `d1 == d2` and we add both, okay. If `d1` or d2 equals offset? no, but if n=1 no. For safety, if d1==offset? It would overwrite q0 after add? Not.

`optimize_pairs` code:
```
def optimize_pairs(h,q,n,precomp,rng,time_limit):
    offset,L,first_idx,second_idx,pai,pj,qs,not_masks=precomp
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    q = np.asarray(q,dtype=float).copy()
    maxsum = float(np.max(q)) if len(q)>0 else 0.0
    best_maxsum = maxsum
    best_h = h.copy(); best_q = q.copy()
    pair_count = len(pai)
    sweeps=0
    tol=1e-12
    while time.time() < time_limit:
       sweeps += 1
       improved = False
       time_break=False
       if pair_count>0:
          order = rng.permutation(pair_count)
          for k in range(pair_count):
             if k and (k & 255)==0 and time.time() >= time_limit:
                time_break=True; break
             idx = order[k]
             i = int(pai[idx]); j=int(pj[idx])
             oi = h[i]; oj = h[j]
             m = oi + oj
             if m < 0.0: m=0.0
             elif m > 2.0: m=2.0
             lo = m - 1.0
             if lo < 0.0: lo=0.0
             hi = m
             if hi > 1.0: hi=1.0
             if hi < lo: hi=lo
             if hi - lo < 1e-12: continue
             data = prepare_pair_data(i,j,h,q,precomp)
             B,A,m2,q0_base,d1,d2,lo,hi = data
             if hi - lo < 1e-12: continue
             t_best, val_best = choose_pair_t(lo,hi,B,A,m2,q0_base,d1,d2,offset,oi)
             if val_best < maxsum - tol:
                 t_best = min(max(float(t_best),lo),hi)
                 new_max = apply_pair_update(h,q,i,j,t_best,B,A,m2,q0_base,d1,d2,offset)
                 if new_max < maxsum - 0.5*tol:
                     maxsum = new_max
                     improved = True
                     if maxsum < best_maxsum - tol:
                         best_maxsum = maxsum
                         best_h = h.copy()
                         best_q = q.copy()
       # recompute q to correct drift
       q = build_q(h,n,offset)
       current_max = float(np.max(q))
       if current_max < best_maxsum - tol:
           best_maxsum = current_max
           best_h = h.copy()
           best_q = q.copy()
       maxsum = current_max
       if time_break:
           break
       if not improved:
           break
       if sweeps % 5 == 0:
           # ensure sum not drifted too much? small
           pass
    # final feasible
    target = 0.5*n
    best_h = make_feasible(best_h, target)
    best_q = build_q(best_h, n, offset)
    best_maxsum = float(np.max(best_q))
    return best_h, best_q, best_maxsum
```

def make_pair_new_q(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    tt = float(t); hy = m - tt
    if tt < 0.0: tt=0.0; hy=m
    elif tt > 1.0: tt=1.0; hy=m-tt
    if hy < 0.0: hy=0.0; tt=m
    elif hy > 1.0: hy=1.0; tt=m-hy
    qt = B + A * tt
    qt[offset] = q0_base + tt*(1-tt) + hy*(1-hy)
    qt[d1] += tt * (1 - m + tt)
    qt[d2] += hy * (1 - tt)
    maxq = float(np.max(qt))
    return tt, hy, qt, maxq

def run(...):
    start = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    hard = start + max(1.0, budget - 5.0)
    if hard > start + budget: hard = start + budget
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c5=1e100; best_n=0
    parent_h=None
    try: parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception: parent_h=None

    has_parent = parent_h is not None and len(parent_h) > 0
    if has_parent:
       n_p = len(parent_h)
       primary_end = min(hard - max(150.0, 0.15*budget), start + 0.65*budget)
       if primary_end < start: primary_end = hard
       h,c,n = try_candidate(parent_h,n_p,rng,primary_end)
       if c < best_c5: best...
    else:
       n_p=96
       h0=random_binary_h(n_p,rng)
       primary_end = min(hard - 100.0, start + 0.8*budget)
       h,c,n=try_candidate(h0,n_p,rng,primary_end)
       best...
    if best_h is None: fallback binary.

    # perturb same best_n
    if has_parent:
       perturb_end = min(hard - max(120.0, 0.12*budget), start + 0.72*budget)
    else:
       perturb_end = min(hard - 80.0, start + 0.85*budget)
    if perturb_end > time.time() + 30:
       amps=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]
       for amp in amps:
          now=time.time(); if now >= perturb_end - 15: break
          allow = perturb_end - now - 5
          if allow < 20: break
          # cap allow
          cap = 60 if amp <=0.05 else 90
          limit = now + min(allow, cap)
          hp = add_perturb(best_h, rng, amp)
          hp2,c2,n2 = try_candidate(hp,best_n,rng,limit)
          if c2 < best_c5: update
    # other ns
    other_deadline = min(hard - 25.0, start + 0.96*budget)
    caps = {95:60,96:80,128:140,190:220,192:220}
    # Choose list excluding best_n, order maybe 96,128,190,95
    for nn in [96,128,190,95]:
       if time.time() >= other_deadline - 20: break
       if nn == best_n: continue
       rem = other_deadline - time.time()
       cap = caps.get(nn,120)
       min_need = max(30, int(cap*0.5))
       if rem < min_need: continue
       # If nn>128 and rem < cap, maybe still if rem > min_need
       allow = min(rem - 10, cap)
       if allow < min_need: continue
       h_in = resample_h(best_h, nn)
       if h_in is None: continue
       h2,c2,n2 = try_candidate(h_in, nn, rng, time.time()+allow)
       if c2 < best_c5: update
    # final random/round if time
    final_deadline = hard - 8.0
    if time.time() < final_deadline - 30 and best_h is not None:
       # try round+perturb best_n
       h_in = round_h_to_target(best_h,best_n)
       h_in = add_perturb(h_in, rng, 0.03)
       h2,c2,n2 = try_candidate(h_in,best_n,rng, final_deadline-2)
       if c2 < best_c5: update
       # if still time, random binary at 96
       if time.time() < final_deadline - 30:
          nn = 96 if best_n !=96 else best_n
          h_rand = random_binary_h(nn, rng)
          h_rand = add_perturb(h_rand, rng, 0.05)
          allow_limit = final_deadline - 2
          h2,c2,n2 = try_candidate(h_rand, nn, rng, allow_limit)
          if c2 < best_c5: update
```

Implement:
```
def rationalize_feasible(h,n,bits=24):
    scale = float(1 << bits)
    h = np.rint(np.clip(h,0,1) * scale) / scale
    target = n/2.0
    # compute sum. Because dyadic, sum exact? Use np.sum.
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    # adjust one index
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       # choose index with room in direction and fractional preferred
       if diff > 0:
           # prefer indices with 0 < h <1; else any h<1
           idxs = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1-1e-12))[0]
           if len(idxs)==0:
               idxs = np.where(h < 1-1e-12)[0]
           order = idxs[np.argsort(1.0-h[idxs])[::-1]]
           for k in order:
               room = 1.0 - h[k]
               if room < abs(diff): continue? We can split? Rounding grid; adjusting one by diff may make h[k] not dyadic? diff is dyadic? Sum of dyadic floats should be exact dyadic multiple of 2^-24; diff multiple of 2^-24. One step may not fit room; choose with enough room.
               h[k] += diff
               diff = 0.0
               break
       else:
           idxs fractional/ any h>0; order by h asc? subtract.
    # If one not enough, distribute across few (dyadic)
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # distribute greedily
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # ensure values multiple? if not due to diff, diff dyadic, h dyadic? Adding dyadic to dyadic exact. good.
    return h
```

def rationalize_feasible(h, n, bits=26):
    scale = float(1 << int(bits))
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float), 0,1)
    h = np.rint(h * scale) / scale
    target = 0.5 * n
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    # To avoid np.sum rounding? It is exact but okay.
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # choose set of indices to adjust
       if diff > 0:
          # prefer fractional then smallest? We want max room
          score = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12), 1.0, 0.0) + (1.0 - h)
          order = np.argsort(-score)
       else:
          score = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12), 1.0, 0.0) + h
          order = np.argsort(-score)
       d = diff
       for k in order:
           if d > 0:
             room = 1.0 - h[k]
             if room <= 0: continue
             add = min(d, room)
             h[k] += add
             d -= add
           else:
             room = h[k]
             if room <= 0: continue
             sub = min(-d, room)
             h[k] -= sub
             d += sub
           if abs(d) <= 1e-15: break
    # snap tiny
    h[np.abs(h) < 1e-15] = 0.0
    h[np.abs(h-1.0) < 1e-15] = 1.0
    # after adjustments, maybe re-round adjusted value? If add dyadic, okay. But due to float addition of dyadic exact? Should be exact if both dyadic and representable. Rounding may have created tiny? Not.
    return h
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_P_ITERS = 24
_F_ITERS = 22
_B_ITERS = 24


def compute_c5_numpy(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    if n == 1:
        return float(np.clip(h[0], 0.0, 1.0) * (1.0 - np.clip(h[0], 0.0, 1.0)) * 2.0)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)) * (2.0 / float(n))


def build_q(h, n, offset):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    q = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    one_minus = 1.0 - h
    for p in range(n):
        hp = h[p]
        if hp != 0.0:
            q[offset - p:offset - p + n] += hp * one_minus
    q[q < 0.0] = 0.0
    return q


def make_precomp(n):
    offset = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    qs = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    first_idx = [np.arange(offset - i, offset - i + n, dtype=int) for i in range(n)]
    second_idx = [offset + i - np.arange(n, dtype=int) for i in range(n)]
    pair_i, pair_j = np.triu_indices(n, k=1, dtype=int)
    not_masks = [qs != i for i in range(n)]
    return offset, L, first_idx, second_idx, pair_i, pair_j, qs, not_masks


def project_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return h
    target = float(target)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s - target) <= 1e-12:
        h[np.abs(h) < 1e-13] = 0.0
        h[np.abs(h - 1.0) < 1e-13] = 1.0
        return h

    a = float(h.min()) - 1.0
    b = float(h.max())
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (a + b)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if val > target:
            a = mid
        else:
            b = mid
    theta = 0.5 * (a + b)
    h = np.clip(h - theta, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(2):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
            for k in order:
                room = 1.0 - h[k]
                if room <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                add = min(diff, room)
                h[k] += add
                diff -= add
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
                    break
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            for k in order:
                room = h[k]
                if room <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                sub = min(-diff, room)
                h[k] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
                    break

    h[np.abs(h) < 1e-13] = 0.0
    h[np.abs(h - 1.0) < 1e-13] = 1.0
    return h


def make_feasible(h, target):
    return project_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), target)


def rationalize_feasible(h, n, bits=26):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    scale = float(1 << int(bits))
    h = np.rint(h * scale) / scale
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            score = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12), 1.0, 0.0) + (1.0 - h)
            order = np.argsort(-score)
            for k in order:
                room = 1.0 - h[k]
                if room <= 0.0:
                    continue
                add = min(diff, room)
                h[k] += add
                diff -= add
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            score = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12), 1.0, 0.0) + h
            order = np.argsort(-score)
            for k in order:
                room = h[k]
                if room <= 0.0:
                    continue
                sub = min(-diff, room)
                h[k] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
                    break
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h[np.abs(h) < 1e-15] = 0.0
    h[np.abs(h - 1.0) < 1e-15] = 1.0
    return h


def random_binary_h(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    rng.shuffle(idx)
    k = n // 2
    h[idx[:k]] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 1 and k < n:
        h[idx[k]] = 0.5
    return h


def smooth_initial_h(n, rng):
    f = 1.0 + 3.0 * rng.rand()
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.rand()
    h = 0.5 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * np.arange(n) / float(n) + phase)
    return make_feasible(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * n)


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return make_feasible(h, 0.5 * n_new)
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return make_feasible(h_new, 0.5 * n_new)


def add_perturb(h, rng, amp):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if amp <= 0.0:
        return make_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
    h2 = h + amp * rng.normal(size=n)
    return make_feasible(h2, 0.5 * n)


def round_h_to_target(h, n):
    r = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    return make_feasible(r, 0.5 * n)


def eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset):
    tt = float(t)
    vals = B + A * tt
    vals[d1] += tt * (1.0 - m + tt)
    vals[d2] += (m - tt) * (1.0 - tt)
    return float(np.max(vals))


def eval_R_scalar(t, m, q0_base):
    tt = float(t)
    return float(q0_base + tt * (1.0 - tt) + (m - tt) * (1.0 - (m - tt)))


def eval_f_scalar(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    return eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset) - eval_R_scalar(t, m, q0_base)


def eval_full_scalar(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    p = eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset)
    r = eval_R_scalar(t, m, q0_base)
    return p if p >= r else r


def ternary_P_min(lo, hi, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset):
    a = float(lo)
    b = float(hi)
    if b - a < 1e-14:
        t = 0.5 * (a + b)
        return t, eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset)
    for _ in range(_P_ITERS):
        c = a + (b - a) / 3.0
        d = b - (b - a) / 3.0
        if eval_P_scalar(c, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset) <= eval_P_scalar(d, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset):
            b = d
        else:
            a = c
    t = 0.5 * (a + b)
    return t, eval_P_scalar(t, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset)


def ternary_f_min(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    a = float(lo)
    b = float(hi)
    if b - a < 1e-14:
        t = 0.5 * (a + b)
        return t, eval_f_scalar(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
    for _ in range(_F_ITERS):
        c = a + (b - a) / 3.0
        d = b - (b - a) / 3.0
        if eval_f_scalar(c, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) <= eval_f_scalar(d, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
            b = d
        else:
            a = c
    t = 0.5 * (a + b)
    return t, eval_f_scalar(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)


def bisection_f_left(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    a = float(lo)
    b = float(hi)
    if eval_f_scalar(a, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) <= 0.0:
        return a
    if eval_f_scalar(b, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) >= 0.0:
        return b
    for _ in range(_B_ITERS):
        mid = 0.5 * (a + b)
        if eval_f_scalar(mid, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) > 0.0:
            a = mid
        else:
            b = mid
    return 0.5 * (a + b)


def bisection_f_right(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    a = float(lo)
    b = float(hi)
    if eval_f_scalar(a, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) >= 0.0:
        return a
    if eval_f_scalar(b, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) <= 0.0:
        return b
    for _ in range(_B_ITERS):
        mid = 0.5 * (a + b)
        if eval_f_scalar(mid, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset) <= 0.0:
            a = mid
        else:
            b = mid
    return 0.5 * (a + b)


def choose_pair_t(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset, t_old):
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        t = lo
        return t, eval_full_scalar(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)

    tP, Pv = ternary_P_min(lo, hi, B, A, m, d1, d2, offset)
    Rmax = q0_base + m - 0.5 * m * m

    cands = [lo, hi, tP, t_old, 0.5 * (lo + hi)]

    if Rmax >= Pv - 1e-10:
        tf, fmin = ternary_f_min(lo, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
        cands.append(tf)
        if fmin < -1e-9:
            f_lo = eval_f_scalar(lo, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
            if f_lo > 0.0:
                left = bisection_f_left(lo, tf, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
            else:
                left = lo
            f_hi = eval_f_scalar(hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
            if f_hi > 0.0:
                right = bisection_f_right(tf, hi, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
            else:
                right = hi
            cands.extend([left, right])

    best_t = tP
    best_val = eval_full_scalar(tP, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)

    for t in cands:
        tt = float(t)
        if tt < lo:
            tt = lo
        elif tt > hi:
            tt = hi
        val = eval_full_scalar(tt, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_t = tt

    span = hi - lo
    if span > 1e-14:
        for frac in (-0.5, -0.25, -0.1, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
            tt = best_t + frac * span
            if lo <= tt <= hi:
                val = eval_full_scalar(tt, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
                if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
                    best_val = val
                    best_t = tt

    return best_t, best_val


def prepare_pair_data(i, j, h, q, precomp):
    offset, L, first_idx, second_idx, pair_i, pair_j, qs, not_masks = precomp
    B = q.copy()
    A = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)

    oi = float(h[i])
    oj = float(h[j])
    if oi < 0.0:
        oi = 0.0
    elif oi > 1.0:
        oi = 1.0
    if oj < 0.0:
        oj = 0.0
    elif oj > 1.0:
        oj = 1.0
    m = oi + oj
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    elif m > 2.0:
        m = 2.0

    non_i = not_masks[i]
    non_j = not_masks[j]
    both = non_i & non_j

    idx = first_idx[i][non_i]
    vals = oi * (1.0 - h[non_i])
    B[idx] -= vals
    idx2 = first_idx[i][both]
    A[idx2] += 1.0 - h[both]

    idxj = first_idx[j][non_j]
    valsj = oj * (1.0 - h[non_j])
    B[idxj] -= valsj
    idxj2 = first_idx[j][both]
    onh = 1.0 - h[both]
    B[idxj2] += m * onh
    A[idxj2] -= onh

    hp = h[both]

    idxi = second_idx[i][both]
    oldi = hp * (1.0 - oi)
    B[idxi] -= oldi
    B[idxi] += hp
    A[idxi] -= hp

    idxjd = second_idx[j][both]
    oldj = hp * (1.0 - oj)
    B[idxjd] -= oldj
    B[idxjd] += hp * (1.0 - m)
    A[idxjd] += hp

    q0_base = float(q[offset]) - oi * (1.0 - oi) - oj * (1.0 - oj)
    B[offset] = -np.inf

    d1 = j - i + offset
    d2 = i - j + offset

    lo = m - 1.0
    if lo < 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
    hi = m
    if hi > 1.0:
        hi = 1.0
    if hi < lo:
        hi = lo

    return B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, lo, hi


def make_pair_new_q(t, B, A, m, q0_base, d1, d2, offset):
    tt = float(t)
    hy = m - tt

    if tt < 0.0:
        tt = 0.0
        hy = m
    elif tt > 1.0:
        tt = 1.0
        hy = m - tt
    if hy < 0.0:
        hy = 0.0
        tt = m
    elif hy > 1.0:
        hy = 1.0
        tt = m - hy

    qt = B + A * tt
    qt[offset] = q0_base + tt * (1.0 - tt) + hy * (1.0 - hy)
    qt[d1] += tt * (1.0 - m + tt)
    qt[d2] += hy * (1.0 - tt)
    qt[qt < 0.0] = 0.0
    return tt, hy, qt, float(np.max(qt))


def optimize_pairs(h, q, n, precomp, rng, time_limit):
    offset, L, first_idx, second_idx, pair_i, pair_j, qs, not_masks = precomp
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).copy()

    if len(q) == 0:
        return h, q, 0.0

    maxsum = float(np.max(q))
    best_maxsum = maxsum
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_q = q.copy()

    pair_count = len(pair_i)
    tol = 1e-12
    sweeps = 0

    while time.time() < time_limit:
        sweeps += 1
        improved = False
        time_break = False

        if pair_count > 0:
            order = rng.permutation(pair_count)
            for kk in range(pair_count):
                if kk and (kk & 255) == 0 and time.time() >= time_limit:
                    time_break = True
                    break

                idx = order[kk]
                i = int(pair_i[idx])
                j = int(pair_j[idx])

                oi = float(h[i])
                oj = float(h[j])
                m = oi + oj
                if m < 0.0:
                    m = 0.0
                elif m > 2.0:
                    m = 2.0

                lo = m - 1.0
                if lo < 0.0:
                    lo = 0.0
                hi = m
                if hi > 1.0:
                    hi = 1.0
                if hi < lo:
                    hi = lo
                if hi - lo < 1e-12:
                    continue

                data = prepare_pair_data(i, j, h, q, precomp)
                B, A, m2, q0_base, d1, d2, lo, hi = data
                if hi - lo < 1e-12:
                    continue

                t_best, val_best = choose_pair_t(lo, hi, B, A, m2, q0_base, d1, d2, offset, oi)
                if val_best < maxsum - tol:
                    t_best = float(t_best)
                    if t_best < lo:
                        t_best = lo
                    elif t_best > hi:
                        t_best = hi
                    tt, hy, qt, new_max = make_pair_new_q(t_best, B, A, m2, q0_base, d1, d2, offset)
                    if new_max < maxsum - 0.5 * tol:
                        h[i] = tt
                        h[j] = hy
                        q[:] = qt
                        maxsum = new_max
                        improved = True
                        if maxsum < best_maxsum - tol:
                            best_maxsum = maxsum
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            best_q = q.copy()

        q = build_q(h, n, offset)
        current_max = float(np.max(q))
        if current_max < best_maxsum - tol:
            best_maxsum = current_max
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_q = q.copy()
        maxsum = current_max

        if time_break:
            break
        if not improved:
            break

    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    best_h = make_feasible(best_h, target)
    best_q = build_q(best_h, n, offset)
    best_maxsum = float(np.max(best_q)) if best_q.size else 0.0
    return best_h, best_q, best_maxsum


def try_candidate(h_in, n, rng, time_limit):
    h_in = np.asarray(h_in, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(len(h_in))
    if n <= 0:
        return h_in, 1e100, n
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h0 = make_feasible(h_in, target)
    if n == 1:
        h0 = rationalize_feasible(h0, n)
        return h0, compute_c5_numpy(h0), n
    pre = make_precomp(n)
    q0 = build_q(h0, n, pre[0])
    try:
        h_opt, q_opt, maxsum = optimize_pairs(h0, q0, n, pre, rng, float(time_limit))
    except Exception:
        h_opt = h0
    h_opt = make_feasible(h_opt, target)
    h_opt = rationalize_feasible(h_opt, n)
    c = compute_c5_numpy(h_opt)
    return h_opt, float(c), n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)

    if budget > 10.0:
        hard = start + max(0.01, budget - 5.0)
    else:
        hard = start + max(0.01, 0.9 * budget)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = 1e100
    best_n = 0

    parent_h = None
    try:
        pv = initial_h_values
        if pv is not None:
            parent_h = np.asarray(pv, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    has_parent = parent_h is not None and len(parent_h) > 0

    if has_parent:
        n_p = int(len(parent_h))
        primary_end = min(hard - max(150.0, 0.12 * budget), start + 0.65 * budget)
        if primary_end < start + 0.01:
            primary_end = hard
        try:
            h_c, c_c, n_c = try_candidate(parent_h, n_p, rng, primary_end)
            if c_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_c
                best_h = h_c
                best_n = n_c
        except Exception:
            pass

        perturb_end = min(hard - max(120.0, 0.10 * budget), start + 0.72 * budget)
        if perturb_end < start + 30.0:
            perturb_end = min(hard - 30.0, start + 0.72 * budget)

        if perturb_end > time.time() + 30.0 and best_h is not None:
            for amp in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20):
                now = time.time()
                if now >= perturb_end - 15.0:
                    break
                allow = perturb_end - now - 5.0
                if allow < 20.0:
                    break
                cap = 60.0 if amp <= 0.05 else 90.0
                limit = min(perturb_end, now + min(allow, cap))
                try:
                    h_pert = add_perturb(best_h, rng, amp)
                    h_c, c_c, n_c = try_candidate(h_pert, best_n, rng, limit)
                    if c_c < best_c5:
                        best_c5 = c_c
                        best_h = h_c
                        best_n = n_c
                except Exception:
                    pass

    else:
        n_f = 96
        h0 = random_binary_h(n_f, rng)
        primary_end = min(hard - max(80.0, 0.08 * budget), start + 0.80 * budget)
        if primary_end < start + 0.01:
            primary_end = hard
        try:
            h_c, c_c, n_c = try_candidate(h0, n_f, rng, primary_end)
            if c_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_c
                best_h = h_c
                best_n = n_c
        except Exception:
            best_h = h0
            best_c5 = compute_c5_numpy(best_h)
            best_n = n_f

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 96
        best_h = random_binary_h(best_n, rng)
        best_h = make_feasible(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
        best_c5 = compute_c5_numpy(best_h)

    other_deadline = min(hard - 25.0, start + 0.96 * budget)
    other_ns = [96, 128, 190, 95]
    caps = {95: 60.0, 96: 80.0, 128: 140.0, 190: 220.0, 192: 220.0}

    for nn in other_ns:
        now = time.time()
        if now >= other_deadline - 20.0:
            break
        if nn == best_n:
            continue
        rem = other_deadline - now
        cap = caps.get(nn, 120.0)
        min_need = max(30.0, 0.5 * cap)
        if rem < min_need:
            continue
        allow = min(rem - 10.0, cap)
        if allow < min_need:
            continue
        try:
            h_in = resample_h(best_h, nn)
            h_c, c_c, n_c = try_candidate(h_in, nn, rng, min(other_deadline, now + allow))
            if c_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_c
                best_h = h_c
                best_n = n_c
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_deadline = hard - 8.0
    if time.time() < final_deadline - 30.0 and best_h is not None:
        try:
            h_in = round_h_to_target(best_h, best_n)
            h_in = add_perturb(h_in, rng, 0.03)
            h_c, c_c, n_c = try_candidate(h_in, best_n, rng, final_deadline - 2.0)
            if c_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_c
                best_h = h_c
                best_n = n_c
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < final_deadline - 30.0 and best_h is not None:
        try:
            nn = 96 if best_n != 96 else best_n
            h_rand = random_binary_h(nn, rng)
            h_rand = add_perturb(h_rand, rng, 0.05)
            h_c, c_c, n_c = try_candidate(h_rand, nn, rng, final_deadline - 2.0)
            if c_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_c
                best_h = h_c
                best_n = n_c
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 96
        best_h = random_binary_h(best_n, rng)
        best_h = rationalize_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        best_c5 = compute_c5_numpy(best_h)
    else:
        best_h = make_feasible(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
        best_h = rationalize_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        best_c5 = compute_c5_numpy(best_h)

    try:
        eval_c = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if np.isfinite(eval_c) and float(eval_c) < best_c5:
            best_c5 = float(eval_c)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(best_c5), int(best_n))
```
